Let's think of implementing continuous profile optimization:
- Represent f on [0,1] as nonnegative piecewise constant with K bins (e.g., 32, 64, 128). Values v_j, integral ∑ v_j Δ=1. This is equivalent to a coarse sequence of length K. Let's look for known optimal continuous/profile score. The problem: minimize sup_{t} ∫ f(x)f(t-x) dx over probability density on [0,1]. This is equivalent to minimizing L∞ norm of convolution. This is a classical problem: "minimum sup norm of convolution of a probability density with itself". There is a result that inf =1/2? But examples? Let's consider a density with two disjoint intervals of masses 0.5 each, far apart? f=δ_{0}+? continuous. If mass on two small intervals near 0 and 1, convolution has three peaks: near 0, 1, 2. To keep each ≤M, mass distribution over two intervals: each interval length maybe; peaks self ~ (mass^2/width). Cross peak ~ product masses. Min balance self and cross. Suppose f = (1/2α)/? Let each of two intervals length L, mass 0.5, amplitude 0.5/L. Self peak ~ (0.5/L)^2 * L =0.25/L. Cross peak ~ (0.5/L)^2 * L =0.25/L if overlap length L? All equal. Score=2M=0.5/L. L≤? If intervals disjoint within [0,1], each L≤0.5. To reduce M need large L, max L=0.5 (adjacent) gives M=0.5, score1. Let's design exact projected subgradient/active-set method:
```
def improve_p(p, time_budget, rng):
    N=len(p)
    best=p,best_score=eval
    eta = 1.0
    for rounds:
       c=convolve(p,p); maxc=c.max(); active=top M indices (maybe based on weights).*
       g=zeros; for k in active: add 2*w_k*p segment reversed with w proportional maybe softmax of top c.
       # line search: try alpha decreasing
       for alpha in [eta, eta*0.5,...]: q=proj_simplex(p - alpha*g/sqrt(N)?). if score improves, accept, eta*=1.2; break; else eta*=0.5.
       if no improvement many, random perturb, or restart with small step.
```
But subgradient steps may be slow. We can use "smoothing + L-BFGS" for faster convergence.

Pseudo:
```
def projected_subgradient(p, max_active=None, eta0=1e-3):
    N=len(p)
    c=convolve_fft(p,p)
    # weights over active set: top K c values with softmax tau_small, or just all weights softmax(c/tau) for smoothed? For exact subgradient, choose active where c > maxc - eps.
    active_mask = c >= maxc - tol (tol = 1e-8*maxc + max(1e-12?))
    if count<1 or >K, take top K.
    g=np.zeros(N)
    # Instead of loop over active (maybe K large), can compute gradient as 2*correlate(w,p) with w normalized over selected active.
    w_selected = softmax(c_active/tau_small) / count? For exact average, w uniform over active. Use uniform.
    w=zeros; w[active]=1/count
    grad = 2*correlate(w,p,valid)
    # grad is ∇smoothmax. For F=2n*max, multiply 2N.
    gradF = 2*N*grad
    # line search
    best_p=p; best_score=score
    eta=eta0
    for l in range(10):
       q=p - eta*gradF
       q=np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if s<eps: break
       q/=s
       sc=evaluate(q)
       if finite and sc<best_score-1e-12:
           return q, sc, eta*1.05
       eta*=0.5
    return best...
```

We need correlate FFT implementation to compute grad_p = 2 * (w ⋆ p) valid length N. Let's implement robustly:
```
def correlate_valid(w, p):
    # w length 2N-1, p length N, returns g_i = sum_{m=0}^{N-1} w_{i+m} p_m
    N=len(p)
    L=1
    conv_len = len(w)+N-1
    while L<conv_len: L*=2
    W=np.rfft(w, L)
    # p reversed
    Pr=np.rfft(p[::-1], L)
    out=np.irfft(W*Pr, L)
    # valid indices N-1:2N-1
    return out[N-1:2*N-1]
```
Check scaling: irfft returns convolution without normalization? numpy FFT: ifft(fft(a)*fft(b)) gives cyclic convolution; zero padding L≥len(a)+len(b)-1 ensures linear. No extra scale. Good.
For N=4806, L=32768? conv_len=9611+4806-1=14416, next 16384, okay.
Convolution p*p: length 2N-1. Use same L≥2N-1; L=power. `c=np.irfft(np.rfft(p,L)**2, n=2*N-1)`. Small imaginary discarded.
Function `convolve_square_fft(p)`:
```
N=len(p); L=1<<( (2*N-2).bit_length() )
C=np.rfft(p,L)**2
return np.irfft(C,n=2*N-1)
```
Need clip tiny negatives from FFT to 0? c should nonnegative; set c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0; maybe negative due roundoff, use np.maximum(c,0). For score max, use max.

Smooth LSE objective:
```
def smooth_max_lse(c, tau):
    c_max=c.max()
    if tau <=0: return c_max
    z=c/tau
    z -= c_max/tau  # if c_max maybe
    vals=np.exp(z-c_max/tau) # stable
    return c_max + tau*np.log(vals.sum())
```
But if tau very small, c-cmax/tau for non-max may underflow to0; fine. Need avoid log(0) when all? max term exp0=1 so sum≥1.
Gradient weights: `w = exp(z)/(sum)` where z=(c-cmax)/tau. Use stable.
Then grad_p = 2*correlate_valid(w, p). This is derivative of smooth max (not multiplied by score factor). Objective can return smooth_max; factor constant. If we want line search objective score, use evaluate_sequence or compute 2N*smooth_max. For optimizer, use smooth_max.

In L-BFGS, objective and gradient should be consistent; we minimize smooth_max. Good.

Block transpose:
```
def apply_block_grad(grad_p, K, N, counts, block_slices):
   grad_q = np.zeros(K)
   for b in range(K):
       grad_q[b] = np.sum(grad_p[slices[b]]) / counts[b]
````

`smooth_max_and_weights(c, tau)`:
```
cmax = float(c.max())
if tau <= 0 or cmax == 0: return cmax, np.zeros_like(c)
z = (c - cmax)/tau
# if z < -745 set -745 to avoid underflow (exp gives 0)
vals = np.exp(np.maximum(z, -745.0))
s = vals.sum()
w = vals/s
return cmax + tau*np.log(s), w
```
If tau very small, z for max=0, others -large; s ~1.

```
def projected_polish(p, deadline, rng, eta=1e-3, max_active=80):
   p = p.copy(); best_score=score_fft(p); best_p=p.copy()
   eta=1e-3
   no_improve=0
   active_tau_scale=0.1
   active_count=50
   while time < deadline:
      c=square_convolve_fft(p); cmax=c.max(); score=2N*cmax
      active = top indices where c > cmax - 1e-8 - 1e-6*cmax? We can select top `active_count` indices always, or all above threshold.
      # weights: softmax over selected with tau = 1e-4*cmax to emulate near max
      sel_c = c[active]
      w_sel = softmax(sel_c / (tau_active)) maybe shift
      w = zeros; w[active]=w_sel
      gp=2*correlate_valid_fft(w,p)
      gF=2*N*gp
      # line search
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(8):
          x=np.log(p+1e-30) - eta*gF
          q=np.exp(np.clip(x, -700,700)); q/=q.sum()
          # Maybe add small noise? If score improves:
          qscore=score_fft(q)
          if qscore < best_score - 1e-12:
              p=q.copy(); best_score=qscore; best_p=p.copy(); eta=min(eta*1.2,5e-3); no_improve=0; accepted=True; break
          elif qscore < score - 1e-12: # accept local even if not global? Could keep best only if global; but to move, accept? We want descend; accept if < current score.
              p=q.copy(); score=qscore; # if < best update; eta*1.05; accepted=True; break
          eta*=0.5
      if not accepted:
         eta*=0.5
         no_improve +=1
         if no_improve>20:
            # perturb
            p += rng.normal(0, 1e-4/N? )? Need relative. p = p + rng.normal(0, 1e-4, N); p=np.maximum(p,0); p/=p.sum()
            if score_fft(p) < best_score: best...; no_improve=0
         # increase active_count for smoother?  active_count=min(active_count*1.5,500); active_tau_scale*=2?
      # slowly decay eta? maybe not
```

```
def make_diverse_inits(K, init_p, N, rng):
   inits=[]
   if init_p is not None: inits.append(downsample(init_p,K,N))
   inits.append(np.ones(K)/K)
   for a in [0.2,0.5,0.8]:
      x=1 + a*(K-1-np.arange(K))/(K-1) # decreasing? high at 0
      inits.append(x/x.sum())
      x=1 + a*(np.arange(K))/(K-1) # increasing
   for beta in [0.5,1,2,4]:
      x=np.exp(-beta*np.arange(K)/K); inits.append(x/sum)
      x=np.exp(-beta*(K-1-np.arange(K))/K)
   # two-step ratios
   split=K//2
   for ratio in [1.2,1.5,2,3]:
      x=np.ones(K); x[:split]=ratio; inits.append(x/x.sum())
      x2=np.ones(K); x2[split:]=ratio; inits.append(x2/sum)
   # random
   inits.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K))*?)
```

Top-level helpers:
- `softmax(x)`: stable. Clamp x to avoid overflow: `x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float); xmax=x.max(); if not finite...; x = x-xmax; exp; sum; if sum==0 return uniform`.
- `build_p_from_q(q, counts)`: `q = np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if small return uniform; q=q/s; return np.repeat(q / counts.astype(float), counts)` counts 1D int length K.
- `downsample_to_q(p, K, N, counts, block_ids)`: `q=np.bincount(block_ids, weights=p, minlength=K); s=q.sum(); return q/s`.
- `square_convolve_fft(p)`: 
```
def square_convolve_fft(p):
    N=len(p); M=2*N-1
    L=1
    while L < M: L <<=1
    spec=np.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.irfft(spec*spec, n=M)
    # due to roundoff, clip small negative
    c = np.where(c < 0, 0, c) # if negative significant? should not; clipping okay for max.
    return c
```
For p sum1, sum c≈1. Clip negative may slightly alter if small.
- `smooth_max_and_weights(c,tau)`: as above.
- FFT correlate:
```
def correlate_valid_fft(w, p):
    N=len(p); Mw=len(w)
    L=1
    while L < Mw + N - 1: L <<=1
    W=np.rfft(w,L)
    P=np.rfft(p[::-1], L)
    out=np.irfft(W*P, L)
    return out[N-1:2*N-1]
```
Need ensure p[::-1] copy? okay.
Could there be a factor? Test mentally with N=2 earlier if no extra scaling. numpy ifft includes 1/L, fft no scale; product as linear convolution scaled 1/L -> correct. Good.

- `build_profile_info(K,N)`: returns counts, block_ids, inv_counts, L_conv, L_corr. We can compute L_conv few: `L_conv=1; while L_conv<2*N-1: <<=1`; `L_corr=1; while L_corr<3*N-2: <<=1`. Use in global context to speed. In `conv_square_fft` use L_conv maybe if N matches. We can make `square_convolve_fft_context(p)` using G_L_CONV and G_N. But for arbitrary N, use generic. For performance, context version:
```
def ctx_square_convolve(p):
   spec=np.rfft(p, G_L_CONV)
   c=np.irfft(spec*spec, n=2*G_N-1)
   c[c<0]=0; return c
```
Global G_L_CONV set.
Similarly `ctx_correlate(w,p)` uses G_L_CORR.
Need careful global variables if N changes; update.

Top-level context setup:
```
G_N=0; G_K=0; G_counts=None; G_inv_counts=None; G_block_ids=None; G_tau=1.0; G_LC=0; G_LCC=0
def set_level_context(K,N,tau):
    global ...
    G_N=N; G_K=K; G_tau=tau
    base=N//K; rem=N-base*K
    counts=np.empty(K,dtype=int); counts[:rem]=base+1; counts[rem:]=base
    G_counts=counts
    G_block_ids=np.repeat(np.arange(K), counts)
    G_inv_counts=1.0/counts.astype(float)
    L=1
    while L < 2*N-1: L <<=1
    G_LC=L
    L=1
    while L < 3*N-2: L <<=1
    G_LCC=L
```
If K=0? no.

```
def upsample_q(q, K_old, K_new, N, p_old=None):
```

```
def full_refine(N, init_p, deadline, rng):
   best_p=init_p; best_score=eval
   # Run level optimizer K=N with a few tau and max_fun maybe 600
```

Projected polish improvements: We can use the current p and FFT. We need top active selection. Instead of sorting full c every iteration (O(N logN) ~9611 log) cheap. Weights: `w_sel=softmax(sel_c/tau_act)` but need scale: sel_c/tau_act ~ large; stable. If tau_act small, weight one. This gradient approximates max.
Then line search. To reduce number of FFT convs in line search, we can use a surrogate: accept if candidate smooth objective with same w? But better exact. We can limit backtracks to 5.
Step eta: Use gradient of score w.r.t p. Maybe use `gF = 2*N*gp`. But when p entries normalized, the descent direction from log parameter might be better: `grad_x = p*(gF - dot(p,gF))`? Wait if we use p-space step q=p-eta gF, projection. Good.
Implement projection by `q = p - eta*gF; q=np.maximum(q,0); q/=q.sum()`. If many negatives, line search fails; reduce eta.

Let's implement p-space with eta schedule and line search.
Pseudo:
```
def projected_polish(p, deadline, rng):
    p=p.copy(); N=len(p); best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_fft(p); current_score=best_score
    eta=1e-3
    min_eta=1e-8
    no_improve=0; stuck_counter=0
    active_k=50
    while time < deadline - 5:
       c=ctx_square_convolve(p); cmax=c.max(); current_score=2*N*cmax
       if current_score<best_score: update
       # active weights
       k=min(active_k, N)
       if k>0:
          idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
          sel_c=c[idx]
          tau_act=max(1e-5*cmax, 1e-15)
          wsel=softmax(sel_c/tau_act)  # shift inside
          w=np.zeros(2*N-1); w[idx]=wsel
          gp=2*ctx_correlate(w,p)
          gF=2*N*gp
          # line search
          accepted=False
          trial_eta=eta
          for _ in range(6):
             q=p - trial_eta*gF
             q=np.maximum(q,0)
             s=q.sum()
             if s <= 1e-15: trial_eta*=0.5; continue
             q/=s
             # Maybe add perturbation if trial_eta < min? no
             csq=qscore_fft(q) (2N*max conv)
             if csq < current_score - 1e-13:
                 p=q; current_score=csq; if csq<best: update; eta=min(eta*1.2,5e-3); accepted=True; no_improve=0; break
             trial_eta*=0.5
          if not accepted:
             no_improve +=1
             eta*=0.7
             if eta < min_eta:
                # random kick
                noise_scale = 1e-4 / N? Actually p entries ~1/N; add noise 1e-5/N? Let's use relative: p += rng.normal(0, 1e-4, N) then project. 1e-4 absolute changes relative 50%; too big. Use 1e-6? p~2e-4, 1e-6 is 0.5%. Good.
                p = p + rng.normal(0, 1e-6, N)
                p=np.maximum(p,0); p/=p.sum()
                current_score=qscore
                if current_score>best_score*1.01: p=best_p.copy(); current_score=best_score
                eta=1e-3
                no_improve=0
                stuck_counter+=1
             # grow active_k for smoother
             active_k = min(active_k*2, 800)
          # optionally decay eta slowly after many? no
       else break
    return p,best_score
```
Need `qscore_fft(q)` use `square_convolve_fft` maybe context if N same. Since p length N same as G_N? In polish after full maybe G_N set. Use generic `fast_score(p)` that uses global if len matches. Define:
```
def fast_score(p):
   N=len(p)
   if N==G_N and G_LC: c=ctx_square_convolve(p)
   else: c=square_convolve_fft(p)
   s=p.sum()
   return float(2*N*c.max()/(s*s))
```
For projected polish, p length N maybe G_N; use context.

Implement `make_counts`:
```
def make_counts(K,N):
    K=int(K); N=int(N)
    if K>=N:
       return np.ones(N,dtype=int)  # K=N
    base=N//K; rem=N-base*K
    counts=np.empty(K,dtype=int)
    counts[:rem]=base+1
    counts[rem:]=base
    return counts
```
If K=1 counts [N]. Good.

`set_level_context`:
```
G_N=N; G_K=K; G_tau=tau

G_block_ids=np.repeat(np.arange(K), counts)
G_inv_counts=1.0/counts.astype(float)
G_LC=1; while G_LC<2*N-1: <<=1
G_LCC=1; while G_LCC<3*N-2: <<=1
```

```
def cur_common(z):
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return None
   zc=np.clip(z,-700,700)
   q=softmax(zc)
   p=build_p_from_q(q, G_counts)
   c=ctx_square_convolve(p)
   val,w=smooth_max_and_weights(c,G_tau)
   return q,p,c,w,val
```

```
def norm_nonnegative(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15: return np.ones(len(x))/len(x)
    return x/s
```

```
def softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if x.size==0: return x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, -700.0)
    xmax=np.max(x)
    if not np.isfinite(xmax): return np.ones_like(x)/x.size
    x=x-xmax
    # clip to avoid underflow? exp of large positive? x-max <=0
    e=np.exp(np.maximum(x, -745.0))
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.ones_like(x)/x.size
    return e/s

def norm_nonneg(x): ...
```

```
def run_level_optimizer(K, init_q, N, deadline, max_fun_total=500, verbose=False):
    if K > N: K=N
    K=int(K)
    q0=norm_nonneg(init_q)
    counts=make_counts(K,N)
    p0=build_p_from_q(q0, counts)
    score0=fast_score(p0)
    best_q=q0.copy(); best_p=p0.copy(); best_score=score0
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_score <= 0: best_score=float('inf')
    # cmax for tau
    c0=square_convolve_fft(p0)
    cmax=float(c0.max())
    if cmax <= 0 or not np.isfinite(cmax): cmax=1.0/N
    z=np.log(np.maximum(q0,1e-15))
    fun_used=0
    tau_factors = (0.2,0.1,0.05,0.025,0.0125,0.00625,0.003125,0.0015625,0.00078125,0.000390625,0.0001953125)
    prev_tau=None
    for fac in tau_factors:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline or fun_used >= max_fun_total: break
       tau = max(fac*cmax, 1e-12)
       if prev_tau is not None and abs(tau-prev_tau)<=prev_tau*0.5: # skip too close? not necessary
       set_level_context(K,N,tau)
       z_clipped=np.clip(z,-700,700)
       no_improve=0
       while time.monotonic() < deadline and fun_used < max_fun_total:
           batch = 25
           if max_fun_total - fun_used < batch: batch = max_fun_total - fun_used
           if batch < 5: break
           try:
              res=minimize(cur_smooth_objective, z_clipped, jac=cur_smooth_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':batch, 'maxfun':batch, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':10})
           except Exception:
              break
           nfev=int(getattr(res,'nfev',batch))
           fun_used += max(1,nfev)
           z_new=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(z_new)): break
           z_new=np.clip(z_new,-700,700)
           q=softmax(z_new)
           p=build_p_from_q(q,G_counts)
           sc=fast_score(p)
           if np.isfinite(sc):
              if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                  best_score=sc; best_q=q.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); z=z_new.copy(); no_improve=0
                  if verbose: print(...)
              else:
                  no_improve += 1
              # update z to best even if not score? Maybe keep res.x if better objective? To avoid bad, set z = z_new if finite? Could wander. Set z to best if improved, else keep best? Use best z? We didn't store best_z. Store best_z too.
           if no_improve >= 4: break
    return best_q,best_p,best_score
```

```
def full_refine(N, init_p, deadline, rng):
    best_p=init_p.copy(); best_score=fast_score(best_p)
    start=time.monotonic()
    # choose full L-BFGS deadline
    lvl_deadline = min(deadline, start + min(300.0, max(60.0, (deadline-start)*0.4)))
    # try base and perturbed if time
    q0=norm_nonneg(best_p)
    p1,sc1=run_level_optimizer(N,q0,N,lvl_deadline, max_fun=400)
    if sc1<best: update
    # If time left, perturb best and run another short

       pq = best_p + rng.normal(0, 1e-4/ N, N)
       pq=norm_nonneg(pq)
       dl2 = min(deadline, time + 60)
       p2,sc2=run_level_optimizer(N,pq,N,dl2,max_fun=200)
       update
    return best_p,best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative steps minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

Method:
- Work with probability vectors (sum = 1).
- Fix the working length N (normally from height_sequence_1, else 4806).
- Run a multigrid-style block profile optimization:
    * Low-resolution periodic-block profiles are optimized by L-BFGS on a
      smoothed max of the square convolution.
    * The best profile is then re-initialized at higher block resolutions.
- After multilevel block optimization, polish the full-length vector with
  L-BFGS on the full parameterization and with an exact projected-subgradient
  line search.
- At the end, test a few harmless length increases / interpolations and return
  the best finite sequence found.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global context for the current multilevel/block parameterization.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
G_N = 0
G_K = 0
G_counts = None
G_block_ids = None
G_inv_counts = None
G_tau = 1.0
G_LC = 0
G_LCC = 0


def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, -700.0)
    xmax = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(xmax):
        return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float) / float(x.size)
    x = x - xmax
    e = np.exp(np.maximum(x, -745.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float) / float(x.size)
    return e / s


def norm_nonneg(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        if x.size == 0:
            return x
        return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float) / float(x.size)
    return x / s


def make_counts(K, N):
    K = int(K)
    N = int(N)
    if K <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if K >= N:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=int)
    base = N // K
    rem = N - base * K
    counts = np.empty(K, dtype=int)
    counts[:rem] = base + 1
    counts[rem:] = base
    return counts


def set_level_context(K, N, tau):
    global G_N, G_K, G_counts, G_block_ids, G_inv_counts, G_tau, G_LC, G_LCC
    K = int(K)
    N = int(N)
    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    G_N = N
    G_K = K
    G_counts = make_counts(K, N)
    G_block_ids = np.repeat(np.arange(K), G_counts)
    G_inv_counts = 1.0 / G_counts.astype(float)
    G_tau = float(tau)

    sz = 2 * N - 1
    L = 1
    while L < sz:
        L <<= 1
    G_LC = L

    sz = 3 * N - 2
    L = 1
    while L < sz:
        L <<= 1
    G_LCC = L


def build_p_from_q(q, counts):
    q = norm_nonneg(q)
    counts = np.asarray(counts, dtype=int)
    if counts.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    vals = q / counts.astype(float)
    return np.repeat(vals, counts)


def square_convolve_fft(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    N = int(p.size)
    if N == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    M = 2 * N - 1
    L = 1
    while L < M:
        L <<= 1
    spec = np.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.irfft(spec * spec, n=M)
    c = np.where(c < 0.0, 0.0, c)
    return c


def ctx_square_convolve(p):
    if G_LC <= 0 or len(p) != G_N:
        return square_convolve_fft(p)
    spec = np.rfft(p.astype(float, copy=False), G_LC)
    c = np.irfft(spec * spec, n=2 * G_N - 1)
    c = np.where(c < 0.0, 0.0, c)
    return c


def correlate_valid_fft(w, p):
    N = int(len(p))
    Mw = int(len(w))
    L = 1
    need = Mw + N - 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    W = np.rfft(w, L)
    P = np.rfft(p[::-1], L)
    out = np.irfft(W * P, L)
    return out[N - 1:2 * N - 1]


def ctx_correlate(w, p):
    if G_LCC <= 0 or len(p) != G_N:
        return correlate_valid_fft(w, p)
    W = np.rfft(w.astype(float, copy=False), G_LCC)
    P = np.rfft(p[::-1].astype(float, copy=False), G_LCC)
    out = np.irfft(W * P, G_LCC)
    return out[G_N - 1:2 * G_N - 1]


def smooth_max_and_weights(c, tau):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    cmax = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
    if tau <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return cmax, w
    z = (c - cmax) / tau
    z = np.maximum(z, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return cmax, w
    w = e / s
    val = cmax + tau * float(np.log(s))
    return val, w


def fast_score(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    N = int(p.size)
    if N == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if G_N == N and G_LC > 0:
        c = ctx_square_convolve(p)
    else:
        c = square_convolve_fft(p)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * N * cm / (s * s))


def cur_common(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    if z.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return None
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0)
    q = softmax(z)
    p = build_p_from_q(q, G_counts)
    c = ctx_square_convolve(p)
    val, w = smooth_max_and_weights(c, G_tau)
    return q, p, c, w, val


def cur_smooth_objective(z):
    res = cur_common(z)
    if res is None:
        return float("inf")
    return float(res[4])


def cur_smooth_gradient(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    res = cur_common(z)
    if res is None:
        return np.zeros(max(1, int(np.size(z))), dtype=float)
    q, p, c, w, val = res
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.zeros(len(z), dtype=float)

    gp = 2.0 * ctx_correlate(w, p)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gp)):
        return np.zeros(len(z), dtype=float)

    weighted = gp * G_inv_counts[G_block_ids]
    gq = np.bincount(G_block_ids, weights=weighted, minlength=G_K)
    dot = float(np.dot(q, gq))
    gz = q * (gq - dot)
    gz = np.where(np.isfinite(gz), gz, 0.0)
    return gz


def downsample_to_q(p, K, N, counts, block_ids):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if p.size != N:
        if p.size == 0:
            return np.ones(K, dtype=float) / max(1, K)
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
        p = np.interp(xn, xo, p)
    q = np.bincount(block_ids, weights=p, minlength=K)
    return norm_nonneg(q)


def run_level_optimizer(K, init_q, N, deadline, max_fun_total=500, verbose=False):
    K = int(K)
    N = int(N)
    if K <= 1:
        N1 = N
        q1 = norm_nonneg(init_q)
        counts1 = make_counts(1, N)
        p1 = build_p_from_q(q1, counts1)
        sc1 = fast_score(p1)
        return q1, p1, sc1
    if K > N:
        K = N

    q0 = norm_nonneg(init_q)
    counts0 = make_counts(K, N)
    p0 = build_p_from_q(q0, counts0)
    score0 = fast_score(p0)
    if not np.isfinite(score0):
        score0 = float("inf")

    best_q = q0.copy()
    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_score = score0
    best_z = np.log(np.maximum(q0, 1e-15))

    try:
        c0 = square_convolve_fft(p0)
        cmax = float(c0.max()) if c0.size else 0.0
    except Exception:
        cmax = 0.0
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        cmax = 1.0 / max(1, N)

    tau_factors = (
        0.20,
        0.10,
        0.05,
        0.025,
        0.0125,
        0.00625,
        0.003125,
        0.0015625,
        0.00078125,
        0.000390625,
        0.0001953125,
    )

    fun_used = 0
    batch = 20 if K <= 512 else 8

    for fac in tau_factors:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline or fun_used >= max_fun_total:
            break
        tau = max(float(fac) * cmax, 1e-12)
        set_level_context(K, N, tau)

        z = np.clip(best_z, -700.0, 700.0)
        no_improve = 0
        bad = False

        while time.monotonic() < deadline and fun_used < max_fun_total:
            rem_fun = max_fun_total - fun_used
            cur_batch = min(batch, rem_fun)
            if cur_batch < 5:
                break

            try:
                res = minimize(
                    cur_smooth_objective,
                    z,
                    jac=cur_smooth_gradient,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": int(cur_batch),
                        "maxfun": int(cur_batch),
                        "ftol": 1e-15,
                        "gtol": 1e-12,
                        "maxls": 10,
                    },
                )
            except Exception:
                break

            used = int(getattr(res, "nfev", cur_batch))
            if used <= 0:
                used = int(cur_batch)
            fun_used += used

            xn = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(xn)):
                bad = True
                break
            xn = np.clip(xn, -700.0, 700.0)

            q = softmax(xn)
            p = build_p_from_q(q, G_counts)
            sc = fast_score(p)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                bad = True
                break

            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = sc
                best_q = q.copy()
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_z = xn.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                if verbose:
                    print(f"    level {K} tau={tau:.3e} score={best_score:.7f}")
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                z = best_z.copy()

            if no_improve >= 4:
                break

        if bad:
            break

    return best_q, best_p, best_score


def make_initial_profiles(K, init_p, N, rng):
    if K <= 1:
        return [np.ones(1, dtype=float)]

    profiles = []
    counts = make_counts(K, N)
    ids = np.repeat(np.arange(K), counts)

    if init_p is not None and len(init_p) == N:
        profiles.append(downsample_to_q(init_p, K, N, counts, ids))

    profiles.append(np.ones(K, dtype=float) / float(K))

    idx = np.arange(K, dtype=float)
    for slope in (-1.0, -0.6, -0.3, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
        x = 1.0 + slope * (idx / max(1.0, float(K - 1)) - 0.5)
        x = np.maximum(x, 1e-3)
        profiles.append(norm_nonneg(x))

    for beta in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        x = np.exp(-beta * idx / max(1.0, float(K)))
        profiles.append(norm_nonneg(x))
        x = np.exp(-beta * (K - 1.0 - idx) / max(1.0, float(K)))
        profiles.append(norm_nonneg(x))

    for freq in (2, 3, 5, 7):
        for amp in (0.35, 0.65):
            x = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * idx / float(K))
            x = np.maximum(x, 1e-3)
            profiles.append(norm_nonneg(x))

    for frac in (0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.55, 0.65, 0.75):
        split = int(round(frac * K))
        if split <= 0 or split >= K:
            continue
        for ratio in (1.25, 1.7, 2.5):
            x = np.ones(K, dtype=float)
            x[:split] = ratio
            profiles.append(norm_nonneg(x))

            x = np.ones(K, dtype=float)
            x[split:] = ratio
            profiles.append(norm_nonneg(x))

    try:
        profiles.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K)))
        profiles.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K) * 0.3))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Keep a manageable but diverse set.
    if len(profiles) > 20:
        profiles = profiles[:20]

    return profiles


def run_multilevel(N, init_p, deadline, rng):
    N = int(N)
    best_p = norm_nonneg(init_p)
    best_score = fast_score(best_p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float("inf")

    K_first = min(16, N)
    if K_first <= 0:
        return best_p, best_score

    counts_first = make_counts(K_first, N)
    ids_first = np.repeat(np.arange(K_first), counts_first)

    shape_q = downsample_to_q(best_p, K_first, N, counts_first, ids_first)
    shape_p = build_p_from_q(shape_q, counts_first)
    shape_score = fast_score(shape_p)

    inits = make_initial_profiles(K_first, best_p, N, rng)
    evaluated = []
    for i, q in enumerate(inits):
        q = norm_nonneg(q)
        p = build_p_from_q(q, counts_first)
        sc = fast_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            evaluated.append((sc, i, q, p))

    evaluated.sort()

    if evaluated:
        sc, _, q, p = evaluated[0]
        if sc < shape_score:
            shape_score = sc
            shape_q = q
            shape_p = p
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p

    num_paths = min(3, len(evaluated)) if evaluated else 0
    if num_paths > 0:
        now = time.monotonic()
        first_total = min(180.0, max(30.0, (deadline - now) * 0.12))
        for j in range(num_paths):
            sc0, _, q0, _ = evaluated[j]
            path_deadline = now + first_total * float(j + 1) / float(num_paths)
            if time.monotonic() >= path_deadline:
                break
            qo, po, sco = run_level_optimizer(
                K_first, q0, N, path_deadline, max_fun_total=140, verbose=False
            )
            if sc0 and np.isfinite(sco):
                if sco < shape_score:
                    shape_score = sco
                    shape_q = qo
                    shape_p = po
                if sco < best_score:
                    best_score = sco
                    best_p = copy_if_finite(po) if False else po
                    best_p = np.asarray(po, dtype=float).copy()

    # Higher resolutions.
    level_list = [K for K in (32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024) if K < N]
    if N <= 512:
        level_list = [K for K in (8, 16, 32) if K < N]

    weights = {}
    maxfuns = {}
    for K in (32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024):
        weights[K] = 1.0
        maxfuns[K] = 800
    weights[32] = 1.0
    weights[64] = 1.3
    weights[128] = 1.7
    weights[256] = 2.2
    weights[512] = 2.8
    weights[1024] = 3.5

    maxfuns[32] = 900
    maxfuns[64] = 800
    maxfuns[128] = 700
    maxfuns[256] = 600
    maxfuns[512] = 480
    maxfuns[1024] = 360

    remaining_w = 0.0
    for K in level_list:
        remaining_w += float(weights.get(K, 1.0))

    for K in level_list:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 15:
            break
        w = float(weights.get(K, 1.0))
        if remaining_w <= 0.0:
            break
        dl = time.Monotonic() if hasattr(time, "Monotonic") else time.monotonic()
        dl = time.monotonic() + (deadline - time.monotonic()) * (w / remaining_w)
        remaining_w -= w

        counts = make_counts(K, N)
        ids = np.repeat(np.arange(K), counts)

        q_shape = downsample_to_q(shape_p, K, N, counts, ids)
        q_best = downsample_to_q(best_p, K, N, counts, ids)

        p_shape = build_p_from_q(q_shape, counts)
        p_best = build_p_from_q(q_best, counts)
        sc_shape = fast_score(p_shape)
        sc_best = fast_score(p_best)

        if np.isfinite(sc_shape) and (not np.isfinite(sc_best) or sc_shape <= sc_best):
            q_init = q_shape
        else:
            q_init = q_best

        qo, po, sco = run_level_optimizer(
            K, q_init, N, dl, max_fun_total=int(maxfuns.get(K, 400)), verbose=True
        )

        if np.isfinite(sco):
            if sco < shape_score:
                shape_score = sco
                shape_q = qo
                shape_p = np.asarray(po, dtype=float).copy()
            if sco < best_score:
                best_score = sco
                best_p = np.asarray(po, dtype=float).copy()

    return best_p, best_score


def projected_polish(p, deadline, rng):
    N = len(p)
    if N <= 0:
        return p, fast_score(p)

    set_level_context(N, N, 1.0)

    best_p = norm_nonneg(p)
    best_score = fast_score(best_p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float("inf")

    cur_p = best_p.copy()
    cur_score = best_score
    eta = 1e-4
    min_eta = 1e-10
    active_k = 60
    active_tau_fac = 1e-5
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
        it += 1
        c = ctx_square_convolve(cur_p)
        cmax = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break
        cur_score = float(2.0 * N * cmax)
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_p = cur_p.copy()

        k = int(min(active_k, len(c)))
        if k <= 0:
            break

        idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
        sel_c = c[idx]
        tau_act = max(float(active_tau_fac) * cmax, 1e-15)
        wsel = softmax(sel_c / tau_act)
        w = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=float)
        w[idx] = wsel

        gp = 2.0 * ctx_correlate(w, cur_p)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gp)):
            break
        gF = 2.0 * N * gp
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gF)):
            break

        accepted = False
        trial_eta = eta
        for _ in range(6):
            q = cur_p - trial_eta * gF
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
            s = float(q.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                trial_eta *= 0.5
                continue
            q = q / s
            qs = fast_score(q)
            if np.isfinite(qs) and qs < cur_score - 1e-10:
                cur_p = q
                cur_score = qs
                if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = cur_score
                    best_p = q.copy()
                eta = min(eta * 1.15, 5e-3)
                accepted = True
                no_improve = 0
                break
            trial_eta *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            eta *= 0.65
            active_k = int(min(active_k * 2, 1200))
            active_tau_fac *= 2.0
            if active_tau_fac > 5e-3:
                active_tau_fac = 5e-3

            if eta <= min_eta:
                # Small kick to escape very shallow subgradient plateaus.
                noise_scale = 1e-3 / float(N)
                kp = cur_p + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, size=N)
                kp = np.maximum(kp, 0.0)
                s = float(kp.sum())
                if s > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(s):
                    kp = kp / s
                    ks = fast_score(kp)
                    if np.isfinite(ks):
                        cur_p = kp
                        cur_score = ks
                        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best_score = cur_score
                            best_p = kp.copy()
                        elif cur_score > best_score * 1.05:
                            cur_p = best_p.copy()
                            cur_score = best_score
                eta = 1e-4
                no_improve = 0

        if it % 50 == 0:
            print(f"  polish iter {it}: best={best_score:.7f} current={cur_score:.7f}")

    return best_p, best_score


def try_length_scalers(p, deadline, max_final_len=10000):
    best_p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).copy()
    best_score = fast_score(best_p)
    N = len(best_p)

    candidates = []
    candidates.append(best_p)

    if np.isfinite(best_score):
        # Threshold tiny values, sometimes helps if the true optimum has zeros.
        tp = best_p.copy()
        tp[tp < 1e-12] = 0.0
        tp = norm_nonneg(tp)
        candidates.append(tp)

    for r in (2, 3):
        M = N * r
        if M > max_final_len or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        q = np.repeat(best_p.astype(float, copy=False), r) / float(r)
        q = norm_nonneg(q)
        candidates.append(q)

    M2 = 2 * N
    if M2 <= max_final_len and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, M2)
        q = np.interp(xn, xo, best_p)
        q = norm_nonneg(q)
        candidates.append(q)

    for q in candidates:
        sc = fast_score(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).copy()

    return best_p, best_score


def full_refine(N, init_p, deadline, rng):
    best_p = norm_nonneg(init_p)
    best_score = fast_score(best_p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float("inf")

    # Full L-BFGS polish from the current full vector.
    full_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + min(300.0, max(60.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.45)))
    q0 = norm_nonneg(best_p)
    p1, s1 = run_level_optimizer(N, q0, N, full_deadline, max_fun_total=320, verbose=True)
    if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score - 1e-12:
        best_score = s1
        best_p = np.asarray(p1, dtype=float).copy()

    # A short warm restart from a slight perturbation can escape a soft plateau.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
        pert = best_p + rng.normal(0.0, 2e-4 / float(N), size=N)
        pert = norm_nonneg(pert)
        dl2 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
        p2, s2 = run_level_optimizer(N, pert, N, dl2, max_fun_total=180, verbose=False)
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = s2
            best_p = np.asarray(p2, dtype=float).copy()

    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400):
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start + max(120.0, float(budget_s) - 90.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(123456789)

    # Initial data / working length.
    init_seq = None
    if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
        try:
            init_seq = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
        except Exception:
            init_seq = None

    if init_seq is None or init_seq.size == 0:
        N = 4806
        init_seq = np.ones(N, dtype=float)

    init_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(init_seq), init_seq, 0.0)
    init_seq = np.maximum(init_seq, 0.0)
    N = int(init_seq.size)
    sm = float(init_seq.sum())
    if N <= 10 or sm <= 1e-300:
        N = 4806
        init_seq = np.ones(N, dtype=float)
        sm = float(N)
    init_p = init_seq / sm

    best_p = norm_nonneg(init_p)
    best_score = fast_score(best_p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float("inf")
    print(f"Initial: N={N}, score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Stage 1: multilevel block-profile construction.
    ml_deadline = min(hard_deadline, start + 0.52 * float(budget_s))
    if time.monotonic() < ml_deadline - 60:
        p_ml, sc_ml = run_multilevel(N, best_p, ml_deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc_ml) and sc_ml < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc_ml
            best_p = np.asarray(p_ml, dtype=float).copy()
    print(f"After multilevel: score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Stage 2: full-length L-BFGS refinements.
    full_deadline = min(hard_deadline, start + 0.78 * float(budget_s))
    if time.monotonic() < full_deadline - 60:
        p_full, sc_full = full_refine(N, best_p, full_deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc_full) and sc_full < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc_full
            best_p = np.asarray(p_full, dtype=float).copy()
    print(f"After full L-BFGS: score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Stage 3 exact projected-subgradient polishing.
    polish_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 30.0, hard_deadline - 75.0)
    if time.monotonic() < polish_deadline - 10:
        p_pol, sc_pol = projected_polish(best_p, polish_deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc_pol) and sc_pol < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc_pol
            best_p = np.asarray(p_pol, dtype=float).copy()
    print(f"After projected polish: score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Stage 4: final harmless transformations / length scalers.
    p_end, sc_end = try_length_scalers(best_p, hard_deadline - 15, max_final_len=10000)
    if np.isfinite(sc_end) and sc_end < best_score - 1e-12:
        best_score = sc_end
        best_p = np.asarray(p_end, dtype=float).copy()

    # Guarantee validity and return a plain Python list of floats.
    best_p = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=float)
    best_p = np.where(np.isfinite(best_p), best_p, 0.0)
    best_p = np.maximum(best_p, 0.0)
    s = float(best_p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        best_p = np.ones(4806, dtype=float) / 4806.0
    else:
        best_p /= s

    out = [float(v) for v in best_p]

    # Final sanity check against the official evaluator, if available.
    try:
        official = evaluate_sequence(out)
        if np.isfinite(official):
            print(f"Official evaluator: score={float(official):.7f}")
        else:
            print("Official evaluator returned non-finite; using fallback uniform.")
            out = [1.0 / 4806.0] * 4806
    except NameError:
        print("evaluate_sequence not visible for final check; returning current best.")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Final evaluator exception: {e}")

    return out
```
